# Skills · P · part 87

873 entries, posterior-credible-region-visualization to postgresql-development-cloudbase. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **posterior-credible-region-visualization**: Use when you have converged MCMC posterior samples (e.g. from emcee over a 2-parameter BBN/NP likelihood) and need joint 68%/95% credible regions as a corner plot plus an upper bound value on a physical parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-credible-region-visualization))
- **posterior-distance-cell-similarity-search**: Use when transferring cell type or continuous fate annotations from a labeled reference scRNA-seq dataset (AnnData/h5ad) to unlabeled query cells, especially across batches or species with batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-distance-cell-similarity-search))
- **posterior-distribution-aggregation**: Use when grid/accept-reject sample posteriors exist per planet and prior option (e.g. plaNETic *_posterior.npy files) and you need weighted summary statistics (median, credible intervals) of derived quantities such as mass-loss rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-distribution-aggregation))
- **posterior-distribution-distance-computation**: Use when querying scRNA-seq cells against a reference embedding (e.g., DIRECTi or scVI latent space) and you need a model-aware similarity metric that accounts for posterior uncertainty rather than Euclidean distance between embedding vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-distribution-distance-computation))
- **posterior-edge-inclusion-thresholding**: Use when you have MCMC posterior samples of inclusion indicators from a MicroBVS DM/DTM Bayesian variable selection run (e.g. prior='MRF_unknown' or Beta-Binomial a=0.1, b=0.9) and must convert them into a discrete selected graph or association set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/posterior-edge-inclusion-thresholding))
- **posterior-effect-size-significance-classification**: Use when a fitted PhenoPath variational model (phenopath_fit) contains posterior interaction coefficients and you must rank and call significant gene-covariate (e.g. MSI-pseudotime) interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-effect-size-significance-classification))
- **posterior-estimator-computation-from-dead-points**: Use when you have a nested sampling run stored as dead points plus the iso-likelihood contours at which they were born (PolyChord &gt;=1.14 'write_dead'=True output), and you need posterior inferences such as logZ or parameter means with standard error estimates on a single run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-estimator-computation-from-dead-points))
- **posterior-inclusion-probability-visualization**: Use when a fitted LUCIDus model (e.g., HCB exposure, incomplete proteomics, childhood BMI z-score) yields bootstrap-based posterior inclusion probabilities and you need to visualize and interpret variable inclusion uncertainty. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/posterior-inclusion-probability-visualization))
- **posterior-label-distribution-estimation**: Use when FFC cluster labels (cluster_obj.cluster_labels after fit(fire_temp=100)) need point-wise confidence quantification, e.g., posterior exclusion probabilities and label entropies to flag transition cells along developmental pseudo-time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-label-distribution-estimation))
- **posterior-marginal-aggregation**: Use when ZODIAC Gibbs sampling (ten parallel chains, 1,000 burn-in + 2,000 sampling epochs) has run on a compound–candidate formula similarity graph and you need per-compound posterior probabilities for every SIRIUS formula candidate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/posterior-marginal-aggregation))
- **posterior-marginalisation-of-read-assignments**: Use when RRBS reads at a multi-sample locus have been assigned to epialleles by bed_fit and you need per-sample epiallele distributions (phi_s) that account for assignment uncertainty rather than hard MAP read labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/posterior-marginalisation-of-read-assignments))
- **posterior-mean-rmse-estimation**: Use when validating or tuning a DE-MC / differential-evolution MCMC implementation on a known target where the true posterior mean is computable, especially the bimodal normal mixture that defeats random walk Metropolis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-mean-rmse-estimation))
- **posterior-memory-harness**: Reconcile uncertain observations about structured agent state (task phases, checkpoints, tool states, plan steps) into globally consistent beliefs using finite relation laws and posterior inference. Use when observations describe related nodes whose composition law matters, when local evidence is ambiguous but redundant constraints exist, when corrections or as-of queries are required, or when one source event must not be counted twice. Not for free-form semantic facts or vector search. ([sudoun/memo_harness](https://github.com/sudoun/memo_harness/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **posterior-parameter-estimation-for-metabolite-distributions**: Use when you have metabolomics data with left-censored missing values (below detection limit) and need to impute them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/posterior-parameter-estimation-for-metabolite-distributions))
- **posterior-predictive-check-visualization**: Use when a jaxspec MCMC (NUTS) fit of a composite model such as Tbabs()*(Powerlaw() + Blackbodyrad()) to an XMM-Newton spectrum has completed and you must verify the posterior actually reproduces the observed 0.3–12 keV counts before reporting parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-predictive-check-visualization))
- **posterior-predictive-checking**: This skill guides validating that the model captures key features of observed data. ([jg-you/bayesian-workflow](https://github.com/jg-you/bayesian-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/posterior-predictive-checking) · ★4)
- **posterior-predictive-outlier-detection**: Use when calibrating pyrosequenced/amplicon methylation levels with MethylCal and suspect aberrant standard-control observations at some CpG-AMP combinations that would bias the fitted calibration curve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/posterior-predictive-outlier-detection))
- **posterior-predictive-simulation-of-count-data**: Use when you have posterior samples from a count-based scRNA-seq model (e.g., BUSseq fits of hematopoietic/pancreas read counts) and need to test whether the model reproduces observed zero-count rates, or whether dropout events must be explicitly modeled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-predictive-simulation-of-count-data))
- **posterior-probability-cell-classification**: Use when demultiplexing pooled single-cell RNA-seq experiments whose cells were labeled with hashtag oligonucleotides (HTOs), and per-droplet sample assignments with error probabilities are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-probability-cell-classification))
- **posterior-probability-confidence-ranking**: Use when merged GCxGC/TOF-MS peak lists (e.g. 76 merged peaks per replicate run) must be assigned to reference-library metabolites and ranked by posterior confidence to produce an identification table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/posterior-probability-confidence-ranking))
- **posterior-probability-cutoff-subtype-classification**: Use when reproducing or comparing cancer molecular subtype classification rates on gene expression data, specifically to quantify how many samples remain unclassifiable under a posterior probability &gt; 0.5 criterion (e.g., DeepCC vs. the 273-gene random-forest CMS classifier). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-probability-cutoff-subtype-classification))
- **posterior-probability-uncertainty-quantification**: Use when BAMM-SC (or DIMM-SC) clustering of droplet-based scRNA-seq data must report which individual cells have uncertain cluster-type assignments, e.g. per-cell posterior probabilities rather than only hard labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/posterior-probability-uncertainty-quantification))
- **posterior-responsibility-computation**: Use when fitting a LUCID latent unknown clustering model on exposures (G), omics data (Z), and outcome (Y) where the EM algorithm must assign each observation a posterior probability of belonging to each latent cluster, especially when sporadic or list-wise missing omics values must be imputed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/posterior-responsibility-computation))
- **posterior-summary-statistics-computation**: Use when you have retained posterior samples from an MCMC run (e.g. CosmoHammer/emcee over WMAP 7 Lambda-CDM parameters) and need means, marginal standard deviations, and a within-tolerance comparison to a reference sampler. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-summary-statistics-computation))
- **posterior-uncertainty-table-generation**: Use when an EMPEROR/reddemcee parallel-tempering run has finished (sim.autorun) and you need to extract median parameter values with asymmetric +/− uncertainties from datalogs into a publication-ready posterior parameter table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/posterior-uncertainty-table-generation))
- **posterior_accuracy_metrics**: Compute posterior moment accuracy metrics for approximate draws against reference posterior summaries. - **posterior_object_contracts**: Validate and normalize posteriordb posterior, model, data, and reference object contracts for benchmarking workflows. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/task-oriented/PaperBench/skills/bam/skill/posterior_db/posterior_object_contracts) · ★357)
- **posterior_sample_approximation**: Fit compact posterior approximations from SBI simulator pairs and emit posterior samples with optimizer traces.
